## 10. CP-0 semantic closure addendum (current byte)

### 10.1 证据口径与关闭规则

本附录是对第 3–9 节的语义补全，不把源码 token、类名、事务注解数量或文件存在冒充 owner 事实。第 9 节曾使用 `SOURCE_ANCHORED_OPEN` 作为生成表在语义复核前的前置标记；完成第 10.8–10.9 节补录后，各行改为 `SOURCE_FACTS_MATCHED_STATIC; DYNAMIC_PROOF_PENDING`。本附录只在下列三种情况下关闭对应记录：

1. `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE`：当前公开命令方法族能定位到其事实 owner，并有同一 owner 的命令/写入、CAS 或锁（若该事实不需要 CAS/锁，记录 `N/A_WITH_REASON`）、以及权威 readback/result 路径；服务只读投影不伪装成写 owner。
2. `KEEP_TASK_READ`、`KEEP_ADAPTER_SUPPORT`、`KEEP_FACADE`、`KEEP_COORDINATOR`：记录该角色的真实职责和边界；command fact、CAS/lock、aggregate readback 用 `N/A_WITH_REASON`，并给出公开入口或调用路径。`N/A` 不是漏查，也不把这些类重新塞进业务聚合。
3. 任一记录只要仍缺少上述事实、只能由方法名或宽 regex 推断，状态保持 `OPEN`，不得因为本附录存在而变成 MATCHED。

### 10.2 五个 facade 的实际 receiver 与目标 execution

五个 facade 的完整 overload 签名和声明行仍以第 4 节为准；下面是从当前实现体逐个展开得到的 receiver 分组。每个分组中的方法名覆盖第 4 节该 facade 的全部 overload；`SalesMenuCommandApi` 等接口重载的 `ownerCommand()` 只改变输入适配，不改变目标 receiver。facade 本身没有 `@Transactional`，没有在委托方法体执行 `JdbcTemplate`；兼容构造器中的 `new targetService(...)` 是 wiring，不是 execution sink。

| facade | receiver/interface | target execution | method family / 当前实现行范围 | CP-0 结论 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `BusinessChannelOwnerService` | `templateService` / `BusinessChannelTemplateService` | `BusinessChannelTemplateService` | `pageTemplates`, `pageStoreTemplateCandidates`, `pageTemplateVisibleStores`, `readTemplate`, `readTemplateCommandContext`, `createTemplate`, `updateTemplate`, `transitionTemplateStatus`（91–228） | 每个 overload 保持 facade FQCN/接口；执行归 template owner |
| `BusinessChannelOwnerService` | `channelService` / `BusinessChannelService` | `BusinessChannelService` | `pageChannels`, `readChannel`, `readChannelCommandContext`, `createChannel`, `updateChannel`, `transitionChannelStatus`, `detachChannelBinding`（144–249） | 每个 overload 保持 facade FQCN/接口；执行归 channel owner |
| `BusinessChannelOwnerService` | `taskReadService` / `BusinessChannelTaskReadService` | `BusinessChannelTaskReadService` | `readChannelWithTemplateProvider`, `findChannelsForBinding`, `listSalesMenuEligibleChannels`, `requireSalesMenuChannel`, `salesMenuChannelBelongsToStore`（180–213） | task-read 只读边界，不转写 owner |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `workbenchReadService` / `CatalogWorkbenchReadService` | `CatalogWorkbenchReadService` | `readWorkbenchContext`, `readNavigation`, `readItems`, `readCategoryCandidates`, `readInventoryDisplayFacts`, `readInventoryTargetDisplayFact`, `readSalesMenuCandidatePage`, `readSalesMenuItemFacts`, `readSalesMenuItemReferenceFacts`（208–254） | task-read/投影入口保持在 facade |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `itemService` / `CatalogItemService` | `CatalogItemService` | `readItem`, `readItemSkus`, `createCatalogItem`, `transitionCatalogItemStatus`, `transitionCatalogItemStatuses`, `saveCatalogItem`, temporary-promotion overloads（258–469 及第 4 节完整 overload 表） | item aggregate execution |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `attributeDefinitionService` / `CatalogAttributeDefinitionService` | `CatalogAttributeDefinitionService` | attribute definition list/create/update/transition（269–295） | definition aggregate execution |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `unitDefinitionService` / `CatalogUnitDefinitionService` | `CatalogUnitDefinitionService` | unit definition list/create/update/transition（299–323） | definition aggregate execution |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `orderOptionDefinitionService` / `CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService` | `CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService` | order-option definition list/create/update/transition（335–361） | definition aggregate execution |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | `categoryService` / `CatalogCategoryService` | `CatalogCategoryService` | category create/update/move/transition（393–421） | category aggregate execution |

| `CatalogOwnerService` | `commandRouter` / `CatalogCommandRouter` | `CatalogCommandRouter` | `write`（386–389） | router 只分派已归属 command，不承担新的事实 owner |
| `InventoryOwnerService` | `availability` / `InventoryAvailabilityService` | `InventoryAvailabilityService` | `readSalesMenuAvailability`（60） | task/projection read；不把 availability 读投影当 inventory mutation owner |
| `InventoryOwnerService` | `target`, `lifecycle`, `bom`, `copy`, `readRouter`, `commandRouter` | 对应 Inventory target/lifecycle/BOM/copy/read/command 类 | 第 4 节 `InventoryOwnerService` 的 68–295 所有 overload 按字段 receiver 展开 | facade 保持；每个 overload 的 receiver 由当前字段及实现体确认 |

| `BusinessEntityService` | `reads` / `BusinessEntityTaskReadService` | `BusinessEntityTaskReadService` | page/list/require read groups（第 4 节对应 `reads` 调用体） | task-read 投影，不改 owner |

这张表的“方法名分组”是导航；CP-0 的逐 overload 记录仍要求使用第 4 节每一条签名，并在 implementation record 中记录实际调用点 `path:line`、receiver 的静态类型和目标方法签名。接口声明、构造器和 facade 自身的 `implements` 不计为 consumer。当前源码没有发现 facade method body 的隐式 self-call；任何实现期把 facade 变为有状态业务实现都必须 STOP 并重新审查。

### 10.3 候选角色型 N/A 与 owner 事实

以下是 89 个候选中非单一 aggregate 的关闭解释；逐类名称和 public 方法仍由第 9 节完整记录。它们不能静默排除，也不能为了填三列而伪造 CAS/lock/readback：

| classification | 当前候选 | 关闭事实与边界 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `KEEP_FACADE` | `BusinessChannelOwnerService`, `CatalogOwnerService`, `InventoryOwnerService`, `BusinessEntityService`, `SalesMenuOwnerService` | 只承接稳定 owner API/command API，receiver 见 10.2；无事务注解和 JDBC execution。CAS/lock/readback 归目标 execution，facade 三列为 `N/A_WITH_REASON=facade does not own mutation`。 |
| `KEEP_COORDINATOR` | `CatalogInventoryCoordinator`, `WorkspaceOperationsCommandService` | 分别从 Catalog→Inventory 与 workspace/organization/iam owner 组合 task/command；公开入口和跨 owner 调用见第 9 节及详设 §3.2B。它们不拥有单一聚合，CAS/lock/readback 必须由被调用 owner 返回；不能为套判据拆进 peer aggregate。 |
| `KEEP_TASK_READ` | `BusinessChannelTaskReadService`, `CatalogTaskReadService`, `CatalogWorkbenchReadService`, `ProductionTagTaskReadService`, `InventoryTaskReadService`, `BusinessEntityTaskReadService`, `OperationsOrganizationTaskReadService`, `OrganizationAssignmentCandidateService`, `OrganizationGroupWorkspaceInitializationTaskReadService`, `OrganizationOverviewTaskReadService`, `OrganizationTaskPathService`, `StoreCandidateTaskReadService`, `ContractTaskReadService`, `PlatformInvitationCandidatesTaskReadService`, `PlatformWorkspaceAccountTaskReadService`, `PlatformWorkspaceInvitationTaskReadService`, `WorkspaceIamSummaryReadService`, `WorkspaceTaskReadService`, `PlatformWorkspaceAdministrationTaskReadService` | 真实职责是组合查询、候选、scope/path 或 projection；第 9 节列出的 public read methods 是结果入口，不是写命令。`command/CAS-lock` 使用 `N/A_WITH_REASON=task-read has no mutation fact`; readback 使用 `N/A_WITH_REASON=returned projection is the read result`。若未来某入口写入 owner，先从 task-read 分类移出并报告，不在拆分中偷偷改变。 |
| `KEEP_ADAPTER_SUPPORT` | `BusinessChannelCommandReceiptService`, `CollaborationCommandReceiptService`, `ExtensionAuditHistoryService`, `ExtensionCommandReceiptService`, `BusinessEntityCommandReceiptService`, `CommercialGroupCommandReceiptService`, `OrganizationAuditHistoryService`, `OrganizationHierarchyCommandReceiptService`, `PlatformAuthenticationService`, `PlatformCommandReceiptService`, `PlatformIamAuditHistoryService`, `SalesMenuOperationRecordService`, `ContractAuditHistoryService`, `ContractCommandReceiptService`, `PlatformWorkspaceAuditHistoryService`, `WorkspaceCommandReceiptService`, `WorkspaceAuditAuthorizationService`, `WorkspaceAuthenticationService`, `WorkspaceCommandAuthorizationService`, `WorkspaceIamAuditHistoryService`, `WorkspaceIamCommandReceiptService`, `WorkspaceLoginRateLimitService`, `WorkspaceOtpRateLimitService`, `WorkspacePasswordRecoveryService`, `WorkspacePasswordResetService` | receipt/audit/auth/rate-limit/authorization support。receipt 的 JSON readback 是协议回放，不是 aggregate authoritative readback；command/CAS/lock 对支持类按职责为 N/A，实际 owner 由其调用方或 owner service 持有。公开嵌套 result/exception 也不被误计为 public method。 |

### 10.4 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE` 的 delegated/direct anchor closure

第 9 节中已按 `N/A_WITH_REASON` 标注的 aggregate 行不是“没有证据”，而是说明 execution service 通过同 module facts/helper 或 read-only projection 完成。当前源码逐项回读的补充锚点如下：

| candidate | command/role fact | CAS/lock 或 N/A | authoritative result/readback | 结论 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CatalogAttributeDefinitionService` | public create/update/transition 在 `.../catalog/application/CatalogAttributeDefinitionService.java:37,47,57`，委托 `CatalogDefinitionFacts` | `CatalogDefinitionFacts.java:500` advisory lock；`518` expected-version compare；`527–533` update/CAS | `CatalogDefinitionFacts.java:541,560` readback；`590,599–605` update/readback | command fact 属 Catalog definition；无需把 facts helper 误排除 |
| `CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService` | `.../CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService.java:36,46,56`，委托同一 `CatalogDefinitionFacts` order-option 方法 | 同上 `CatalogDefinitionFacts.java:500,518,527–533,590,599–605` | 同上 readback anchors | order-option definition 在同一 Catalog definition owner 内 |
| `CatalogUnitDefinitionService` | `.../CatalogUnitDefinitionService.java:52,61,76`，委托 `CatalogUnitDefinitionFacts` | `CatalogUnitDefinitionFacts.java:69,255` lock；`125–134,150–165` CAS/status | `CatalogUnitDefinitionFacts.java:99,139,165,325–327` | unit definition owner facts 已定位 |
| `InventoryAvailabilityService` | `.../inventory/application/InventoryAvailabilityService.java:77` 是 read-only sales-menu availability projection | `N/A_WITH_REASON=read projection; no mutation/CAS/lock` | 同一 read method 及其 row mapping（约 `161,216–231`）是 projection result | 不把 read projection 拆成伪 aggregate command |
| OrganizationVisibilityService | public visibility/session-scope policy read at organization/application/OrganizationVisibilityService.java:28,95,109,295 | N/A_WITH_REASON=read-only policy; no command/CAS/lock owner | result methods at 28,95,109,295 are policy readbacks | KEEP_TASK_READ |
| WorkspaceAssignmentScopeService | public active assignment-scope lookup at workspace-iam/application/WorkspaceAssignmentScopeService.java:19 | N/A_WITH_REASON=read-only scope lookup; no command/CAS/lock owner | requireActiveScope@19 is the typed scope result | KEEP_TASK_READ |
| `WorkspaceUserService` | `.../workspace-iam/application/WorkspaceUserService.java:74,89,129,170,191,250,335,369` 是 task/scope/candidate read | `N/A_WITH_REASON=read-only/task scope` | 各方法返回的 scope/candidate/page/detail result | 其跨 owner lookup 依赖不改变 facts owner |
| PlatformWorkspaceService | list@35 and detail@41 are task-read facets; initializeCommercialGroup@47 calls the organization owner command | N/A_WITH_REASON=coordinator; no aggregate fact root | initializeCommercialGroup@47 readback plus list/detail task results | KEEP_COORDINATOR |

其余 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE` 行均已有直接 public command、receipt/CAS/lock 和 result anchor（第 9 节对应列）；其中 `NONE_DIRECT` 只允许用上述“同 module facts / 明确 read-only role”方式关闭。若 CP-0 逐点复核发现某行不能落在一个事实根，必须改为 `OPEN`，不得用 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE` 的初始标签维持结论。

### 10.5 facade/target self-call 与外层事务矩阵

self-call 不是“代码中出现同名 token”。本次矩阵采用以下闭合口径：只统计同一顶层类、无 receiver qualifier 的方法调用；排除 nested record/class/interface 的方法体、构造器、接口声明、`this.`/其它对象限定调用和方法引用。对每个带事务注解的未来 execution target 记录 `self-call=NONE_CONFIRMED` 或调用点与外层事务属性；不能解析的候选记录 `OPEN`，不以启发式扫描关闭。

当前已逐点核实的关键边界：

- 五个 facade：`self-call=NONE_CONFIRMED`；facade 无 `@Transactional`，目标事务属性保留在目标 bean/原 execution 点。
- `InventoryAvailabilityService.readSalesMenuAvailability@77`：`@Transactional(readOnly=true)`，没有同类 self-call；它是只读 projection，直接 JDBC read/result mapping 不构成 mutation boundary。
- `CatalogAttributeDefinitionService` 与 `CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService` 的 public mutation 只是转入 `CatalogDefinitionFacts`；事务/lock/CAS/readback 的 owner 锚点见 10.4，不能把 package-private helper 调用误当 facade self-call。
- `CatalogUnitDefinitionService` 的 public mutation 对应 `CatalogUnitDefinitionFacts` lock/CAS/readback；同理按 facts receiver 记录，不按同名 token 记录。
- `CatalogItemService`、`CatalogCopyService`、`InventoryBomService`、`InventoryCopyService`、`OrganizationHierarchyService`、`ContractCommandService`、`WorkspaceInvitationService`、`WorkspaceRoleService` 等含 overload/helper 调用的类：CP-0 矩阵保留每个真实 unqualified call 的 caller method、callee、外层 `@Transactional`/`TransactionTemplate` 属性；qualified helper、nested type 与 private data mapper 只作排除证据。当前 36-target 的逐点 source register 已见 10.5B；拆分后的 execution family 仍必须在所属批次证明运行期语义。

因此本附录不把“所有方法都没有 self-call”作为假设；10.5A 保留此前八个 target 的历史详细子集，10.5B 才是当前 36 个未来 aggregate execution target 的完整 source register。动态 proxy、传播、锁/CAS、幂等、回滚和 authoritative readback 仍按所属批次证明，不能由任一静态 register 代替。

### 10.5B · complete current future-execution target transaction/self-call register

本节是对 10.5A 历史详细子集的当前字节补全。scope 取第 12.2A 中 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE` 的 33 行；`OrganizationVisibilityService` 与 `WorkspaceAssignmentScopeService` 当前分类为 `KEEP_TASK_READ`，`PlatformWorkspaceService` 当前分类为 `KEEP_COORDINATOR`，它们不属于未来 aggregate execution target，故不放入本表。

### 10.6 raw-SQL / fragment 的真实执行闭包

当前全量边界扫描中，真正的公开 raw-SQL boundary 是：

- `.../salesmenu/infrastructure/SalesMenuRepository.java:21` `query(String, RowMapper<T>, Object...)`；`:23` `update(String, Object...)`；
- `.../salesmenu/infrastructure/JdbcSalesMenuRepository.java:88,93` 对应实现；
- sales-menu application 的调用点按类分组为 `SalesMenuOperationRecordService:207`、`SalesMenuItemService:325,342,347,390,394,399,404,408,458,611,682,715,777,783,789,848,907,1139,1151,1507,1529,1535,1545,1550,1555,1569,2033`、`SalesMenuManualSaleService:193,214,306,404,542`、`SalesMenuSectionService:162,166,186,209,345,443,449,455,670`、`SalesMenuDefinitionService:146,236,245,249,282,291,310,321,338,355,463,532,539,541,551,571,576,594,607,621,636,654,677,689,798,906`、`SalesMenuPublicationService:155,168,191,253,574,592,606,676,716,739,754,816,827,839,1164`。

这些调用点不是全部同形：`query(String...)`/`update(String...)` 的 generic sink、private query helper、nested `PreparedStatement` override、以及业务 SQL 直接调用必须在 CP-0 分别标为 `raw-boundary`、`private-helper`、`JDBC-callback` 或 `business-sql-to-be-moved`。宽扫描中的误报不能删除；必须有逐点处置。最终每条 `raw-boundary` 都要在所属 execution 批次变为业务参数方法：读侧 filter/sort/page/target；写侧聚合/命令对象/字段值等，条件选择与组合留在 persistence 内。

其它当前 source anchor：`JdbcGroupWorkspaceRepository.java:25,48` 是具名 workspace repository execution；`PlatformWorkspaceAuditHistoryService.java:52` 与 `WorkspaceCommandReceiptService.java:36,48,62` 是 workspace audit/receipt 支撑入口。它们的 role 与 M-08 三文件边界在 B3/B4 矩阵中分别记录，不能用“没有 `String sql` public signature”当作无 SQL 事实。

### 10.7 CP-0 关闭状态

本附录把“全集已枚举”“source facts 已记录”和“动态行为已证明”分开。当前 source facts 已按 89 行逐行记录：facade receiver/target、角色型 N/A 理由、delegated anchors、facade/read-only self-call 排除、raw/fragment/wrapper 调用点、candidate test-gap disposition 均有对应段落。仍必须由 fresh reviewer 复核、并由所属批次动态证明的项目是：每个 facade overload 的传播处置是否与当前 consumer 完全一致、每个 target transaction family 的真实 self-call/outer transaction 行为、每个 raw/fragment/wrapper 调用点的最终 persistence owner，以及锁/CAS/幂等/回滚/authoritative readback 的运行期成立性。

当前只读自一致性计数为：`candidateRows=89`、`transactionBearingFileRows=68`、`futureExecutionTargetRows=33`、`rawSqlClosureRows=47`、`methodFamilyRows=366`；分类分布为 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE=33`、`KEEP_ADAPTER_SUPPORT=25`、`KEEP_FACADE=5`、`KEEP_TASK_READ=23`、`KEEP_COORDINATOR=3`。这些数字只校验本证据各表的当前字节闭合，不是行为覆盖、成本、验收或准入分母。

在这些补充记录被 fresh reviewer 接受前，状态保持 `SEMANTIC_CP0_CLOSURE=OPEN`，B1 不得开始。此附录不把“有一份表”冒充 MATCHED，也不替代后续每批的 focused proof、三维对账或最终全量 acceptance。

## 11. CP-0 overload-to-receiver expansion (current byte)

为避免第 10.2 节的族级分组被误读为逐点证据，下面保留当前五个 facade 每一个 public method body 的实际 delegate。左侧行号是当前源码中的声明起始行，完整签名在第 4 节同名 facade 段逐行列出；右侧是从方法体解析并人工回读确认的静态 receiver 与目标方法。构造器单独属于 wiring，静态 validator 单独属于稳定公开工具面；BusinessEntityService 的嵌套 exception constructor 不属于 facade overload。

### 11.1 BusinessChannelOwnerService

### 11.6 调用方 receiver 传播

第 5 节的引用全集是 locator；CP-0 的传播规则是：对每个上述 facade overload，逐一从当前 Java source 的 import、字段声明、构造注入和调用表达式确认 receiver/interface 类型，再把该 overload 的消费者记录到同一行。测试中的直接 concrete construction 只保留为 compatibility consumer；edge controller、其它 owner、`ContractProblemAdvice` 与 test consumer 若引用 facade FQCN/interface，则在拆分时只允许更新内部 target wiring，不得删除稳定 facade。当前实现没有 Spring 配置文件通过名称覆盖这些构造器；唯一 bean 是 facade 与其 `@Service` target。出现同类型多 bean、需要 `@Primary`/fallback 才能编译时必须 STOP。

## 12.0B BusinessEntityService current-byte complete overload binding

本节是对 §12.0A（caller locator）与 §11.4/§11.4A（delegate/target locator）的合并闭包。基准是当前 `BusinessEntityService.java` 字节，而不是历史记录中的 declaration label。共发现 68 个顶层 public method overload；constructor、private helper、public nested type 不混入本表。`NONE_CONFIRMED` 只表示在当前 exact-type consumer 搜索域未命中，不是删除理由；`KEEP_FACADE` 是当前保持/修改/删除处置，B4–B16 仍需按相同 caller、事务、锁/CAS、幂等、回滚、readback 证据复核。

表内 facade interface 是当前 class `implements` 的接口中与该入口相匹配的接口；`NONE_FACADE_INTERFACE` 表示该 overload 不是接口声明。target receiver static type 与 target execution declaration 均来自当前源码声明和方法体；unqualified self-dispatch 被明确标记，不能被误当 bean proxy。

当前 raw scan 的真正 closure 不是“出现了 26 个宽命中”，而是以下逐调用点规则：

- `SalesMenuRepository.java:21` 与 `:23` 是公开 generic boundary；`JdbcSalesMenuRepository.java:88,93` 是对应实现，归 `sales-menu` execution，状态 `TO_BE_REPLACED_BY_BUSINESS_BOUNDARY`。
- `SalesMenuOperationRecordService:207`、`SalesMenuManualSaleService:193,214,306,542`、`SalesMenuDefinitionService:146,236,245,249,282,291,310,321,338,355,463,532,539,541,551,571,576,594,607,621,636,654,677,689,798,906`、`SalesMenuItemService:325,342,347,390,394,399,404,408,458,611,682,715,777,783,789,848,907,1139,1151,1507,1529,1535,1545,1550,1555,1569,2033`、`SalesMenuPublicationService:155,168,191,253,574,592,606,676,716,739,754,816,827,839,1164`、`SalesMenuSectionService:162,166,186,209,345,443,449,455,670`：每个调用点的 owner 是其所在 sales-menu service；execution target 是该 service 的当前 method/private helper；B4–B16 迁移时由该 execution point 的稳定 key 指向目标 persistence method。
- 上述列表中以 SQL 变量传入 generic `query` 的点（`SalesMenuItemService:848,907`、`SalesMenuManualSaleService:404`、`SalesMenuPublicationService:816`）标为 `RAW_SQL_VARIABLE_BOUNDARY`；含动态表/列选择的 `SalesMenuItemService:777,783,789` 标为 `DYNAMIC_SQL_BRANCH`；其余直接 literal `repository.query/update` 标为 `BUSINESS_SQL_TO_BE_MOVED`。private helper 不是豁免，只是执行点继承其 public caller 的 owner/事务/目标；nested `PreparedStatement` callback 不被误分类为 public raw boundary。
- `workspace/PlatformWorkspaceAuditHistoryService.java:52` 是 audit projection read；`WorkspaceCommandReceiptService.java:35,36,48,62` 是 receipt lock/read/write support；`WorkspaceAdministrationService.java:87,126,157,180,276,326,361,376` 是 workspace administration execution。它们按候选表的 aggregate/task-read/support 角色分别记录，不因不具备 sales-menu 的 generic signature 而从 SQL inventory 中消失。

因此当前 CP-0 具备 source-level 的逐调用点 owner 和 execution point 定义；B4–B16 才负责把每一条 `TO_BE_REPLACED` 实际改为业务级参数并作 before/after capture。对调用点无法确定 owner 或跨 module task-read 没有既定 owner 的情况，状态仍为 `OPEN` 并报告 Dexter，不以“先放到某个 persistence”代替决定。

## 12.2 CP-0 static fact closure matrix

本节把 89 个候选从“有锚点”提升为可复核的 source-fact disposition；动态行为仍由所属批次证明，不能用本节替代 focused proof。`SOURCE_FACTS_MATCHED_STATIC; DYNAMIC_PROOF_PENDING` 的含义是：当前源码已给出该候选的角色/命令事实、CAS/lock 或精确 N/A、result/readback 路径；事务代理、传播、并发、回滚和业务 readback 的运行期证明仍是后续批次责任。

| classification | rows | static closure rule | source evidence |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE` | 33 | 第 9 节同一行列出 public command/read method、CAS/lock/receipt 与 result/readback；3 个 `NONE_DIRECT` 行由第 10.4 节逐项补齐，不以方法名或 token 关闭 | 第 9 节逐行；第 10.4 节 `CatalogAttributeDefinitionService`、`CatalogOrderOptionDefinitionService`、`CatalogUnitDefinitionService` 及其 3 个真实行 |
| `KEEP_ADAPTER_SUPPORT` | 25 | 每行按 receipt/audit/auth/rate-limit/support 的职责关闭；command/CAS/lock/aggregate authoritative readback 写 `N/A_WITH_REASON`，协议回放 result 单列，不冒充 aggregate readback | 第 9 节逐行；第 10.3 节 support 角色说明 |
| `KEEP_FACADE` | 5 | 每行由第 11.1–11.5 的完整 public method-body delegate、receiver/interface、target execution 与稳定 facade 保留规则关闭；facade mutation 三元组为 `N/A_WITH_REASON=facade does not own mutation` | 第 11.1–11.5；第 11.6 的实际引用处置 |
| `KEEP_TASK_READ` | 23 | 每行按 task-read/projection/candidate/scope职责关闭；无 command/CAS/lock，返回 projection 即 read result，写 `N/A_WITH_REASON` 并保留 exact public result anchor | 第 9 节逐行；第 10.3 节 task-read 角色说明 |
| `KEEP_COORDINATOR` | 3 | 每行逐项记录跨 owner 调用与被调用 owner 的返回；自身不拥有 peer aggregate 的 CAS/lock/readback，写 `N/A_WITH_REASON=coordinator delegates owner command/read` | 第 9 节 `CatalogInventoryCoordinator`、`WorkspaceOperationsCommandService`、`PlatformWorkspaceService`；第 10.3 节 |
| **TOTAL** | **89** | **33 + 25 + 5 + 23 + 3 = 89；零静默排除** | 第 3 节候选全集与第 9 节逐行记录 |

对 `KEEP_SINGLE_AGGREGATE`，第 9 节非 `NONE_DIRECT` 的每一行已经在同一行给出具体 public method 与 source anchor；第 10.4 的 3 行是唯一 delegated/direct 补录集合。对四类 N/A 角色，N/A 是职责事实而不是缺证据。若后续复核发现任何一行不能满足其 classification 的 closure rule，该行必须回退为 `OPEN`，不得以本汇总表强行通过。

### 12.2A row-wise static closure register

This is the row-wise static closure register for all 89 candidates in section 9, not only the non-direct rows. ## 12.3 facade consumer / propagation disposition

facade 的结构变更只允许发生在 facade 内部 target wiring；外部 consumer 不得被静默改为直接依赖新 execution class。当前字节以 exact-type 形态复核出的消费者处置如下：

| facade | source consumer set | disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `BusinessChannelOwnerService` | `BusinessChannelOwnerContractTest.java`、`BusinessChannelSalesMenuOwnerTest.java`、`BusinessChannelCommandQueryTest.java`；生产 edge 无 direct concrete consumer | 测试 concrete construction 只更新 constructor/wiring；owner API consumer 保持 facade/interface |
| `CatalogOwnerService` | catalog owner integration/read-model/asset/copy/dictionary/page/sales-menu tests 与 `CatalogInventoryReadTransactionTopologyTest.java` | 保留 `CatalogOwnerApi` 与 `CatalogTemporaryPromotionOwner` facade；测试 direct construction 只更新 wiring |
| `InventoryOwnerService` | inventory owner/ledger/copy/BOM/lifecycle/sales-menu tests、catalog cross-owner tests、`CatalogInventoryCoordinator.java` | coordinator 继续经 `InventoryOwnerApi`；测试 construction 只更新 wiring，不直连 target |
| `BusinessEntityService` | operations organization/contract controllers、`ContractProblemAdvice.java`、organization/workspace-iam task services、对应 controller/owner tests | edge 与跨 module 继续保留稳定 facade/FQCN；公开嵌套 exception 的 FQCN 映射不移动 |
| `SalesMenuOwnerService` | `SalesMenuOwnerServiceOwnerApiTest.java`、`SalesMenuOwnerServiceQueryTest.java`、`SalesMenuOwnerServiceReadModelTest.java`、`SalesMenuOwnerServiceOrderingTest.java` | sales-menu tests 保留 facade concrete compatibility construction；无生产 direct consumer需改为 target |

本表与第 11.1–11.5 的逐 overload delegate 表共同构成传播矩阵：前者覆盖 facade 的外部 exact-type consumer，后者覆盖每个 public overload 的 receiver/target。`rg` 只能作定位；每一条列入的测试/生产 consumer 均已按 import、字段/参数静态类型和构造表达式人工复核，未将 `BusinessEntityService` 的嵌套异常引用误算为 facade consumer。

本表的 OPEN 是有意保留的缺口状态，不是 CP-0 通过标记。CP-1 与各 execution 批次必须以实际 test method、业务 oracle、写后权威 readback 和适用事务风险证据逐行关闭；未能找到适用 oracle 的方法族必须继续 OPEN，并阻止对应结构移动。

### 12.4A · exact test-method locator disposition

为避免把候选级文件引用冒充测试定位，当前 366 个 method-family row 的独立测试方法定位统一记为 `NONE_FOUND`：第 12.4 表的 `candidate source locator (not test locator)` 不计入该字段。`NONE_FOUND` 表示 CP-0 尚未接受 `test/path.java:line` 级的正向/负向 oracle 映射，不表示仓内绝对不存在可复用测试；CP-1 的行为钉住步骤必须按 `candidate + method family + declaration line` 逐行补入 exact test method locator，无法找到的行继续以 `NONE_FOUND` 留痕并保持 OPEN。

### 12.4B · CP-0 test coverage boundary

`12.4` is an inventory of the current test-evidence state and gaps, not a claim that every method family already has behavior coverage. A class-name reference, a candidate test-file reference, or a method-name token is only a navigation clue; it is not an accepted positive oracle, negative oracle, authoritative readback, or transaction-risk proof. Therefore `NONE_FOUND` is the conservative current state for the exact method/oracle mapping and deliberately leaves the row open. The CP-0 gate is closed by complete enumeration and explicit gap disposition; CP-1 must replace each applicable row with an exact test method, real fixture/action, business oracle, post-write authoritative readback, and applicable transaction-risk proof before the corresponding structural move. If no behavior oracle is applicable, CP-1 must record `N/A_WITH_REASON` against the actual method family rather than silently treating absence as coverage.

The current read-only association scan is retained only as a navigation aid, not as a coverage measure: a method-name occurrence can be a helper call, a fixture builder, a mock setup, or an unrelated overload. It is therefore not converted into a count or percentage. This prevents the earlier `NONE_FOUND` label from being misread as a claim that no tests mention those methods.
